For each risk tier, replace standalone occurrences with \"Risk (SeverityNote)\"\n    for risk, severity in SEVERITY_MAP.items():\n        # Match the risk tier that is NOT already followed by the severity note\n        # Use negative lookahead to avoid double-applying\n        pattern = rf'\\b{re.escape(risk)}\\b(?! \\({re.escape(severity)}\\))'\n        replacement = f'{risk} ({severity})'\n        content = re.sub(pattern, replacement, content)\n\n    return content\n\ndef remove_line_seg_array(content):\n    \"\"\"Remove all lineSegArray elements and their contents.\"\"\"\n    #
